# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#  METADATA Data Description:  
#
#  ABSTRACT:  This data set contains water-quality data for radon 
#	      for wells included in a report that summarizes   
#	      the quality of water from wells sampled in principal 
#             aquifers of the United States by the NAWQA program 
#	      from 1991-2010. The data were retrieved from the NAWQA
#             internal Data Warehouse in August 2010. 
#	
#
#  SUPPLEMENTAL INFORMATION:
#   	PROCEDURES USED:
#	PROCESS STEP:
#	  Radon was retrieved from the NAWQA internal Data Warehouse (DWH) in
#	  August 2010.
#
#	  The data were retrieved using the decadal sample identifier
#         "FULL NETWORK CYCLE"; this parameter identifies samples collected 
#         during the decadal sampling of all wells in a groundwater
#         study as part of NAWQA Groundwater Status and Trends assessments.
#
#         Data for some sites, for which the decadal sample identifier
#         was not populated, were retrieved using a condition that specified
#         "Staid" or "Su Code" equal to the station identifiers or ground-
#         water studies of interest. These sites included the DWGS sites
#         and sites for other studies that were not considered part of 
#         NAWQA Groundwater Status and Trends decadal assessments.
#
#	PROCESS_STEP
#	  The 15-digit station identifier was replaced with a sequential
#	  well identifier.
#	     
#
# Related_Tabular_Data_Sets:  
#	pasum_pub.archival_file_summary.txt

#	pasum_pub.wellchars.txt		
#	pasum_pub.field.txt

#	pasum_pub.vocs-CAL.txt

#

#

#	V_82303_Radon-222
#       R_76002_Radon-222_2-sigma_combined_uncertainty

#	
#	
#     DATA TYPES: 
#
#	Note:  The row in the data file that follows the row of variable 
#	names describes the width of the field and the data type. 
#	Code:	Variable Type		Variable Length
#	----------------------------------------------------------------
#	s 	text variable		numeric value preceding code 
#	n	numeric variable	numeric value preceding code
#									 
#	For example: 15s indicates a 0- to 15-character text variable whereas
# 	9N indicates a 0- to 9-digit numeric variable.
#
#
#     DATA SOURCES:
#	DWH	= USGS NAWQA data warehouse, accessible publicly at: 
#                    http://water.usgs.gov/nawqa/data
#
#
#
#--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#  METADATA Detailed documentation of dataset elements:
#--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#
#	DATA FIELD GROUP: SITE AND SAMPLE IDENTIFICATION
#
#       DATA FIELD LABEL: well identifier
#       Data Field Description: sequential identifier; the first 4 letters
#		are abbreviations representing the principal aquifer or group 
#		of principal aquifers in which the well is located for aquifers
#		that are the subjects of regional principal aquifer assessments,  
#		which are published in separate U.S. Geological Survey Circular
#		reports, as follows:
#		DENV = Denver Basin aquifers, Circular 1357
#		UFSF = Upper Floridan and Surficial aquifers, Circular 1355
#		GLAC = Glacial aquifer, Circular 1352
#		HPGW = High Plains aquifer, Circular 1337
#		METX = Mississippe Embayment-Texas coastal uplands aquifer
#		  system and Mississippi River Valley Alluvial aquifer, 
#		  Circular 1356
#		NACP = Northern Atlantic Coastal Plain aquifer system, 
#		  Circular 1353
#		PBVR = Piedmont, Valley and Ridge, Blue Ridge, and Early
#		  Mesozoic basin aquifers, Circular 1354
#		SWPA = Basin and Range basin-fill, California Coastal basin, 
#		  Central Valley, and Rio Grande aquifers, Circular 1358 
#		WEVO = Columbia Plateau, Snake River Plain, and Hawaiian
#		  aquifers, Circular 1359
#		OTHR = All other aquifers
#	Data Units: not applicable
#       Data Source: calculated
#       Retrieval Date: not applicable
#
#       DATA FIELD LABEL: sampling_date
#       Data Field Description: measurement date for radon from DWH item
#		"Result Datetime"
#	Data Field Length/Type: 18s
#	Data Units: not applicable
#       Data Source: DWH
#       Retrieval Date: August 2010
#
#--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#		
#	DATA FIELD GROUP: RADON
#
#       DATA FIELD LABEL: See details below in this section
#	Data Field Length/Type: 1s or 12n
#	Data Units: picocuries per liter
#       Data Source: DWH
#       Retrieval Date: August 2010
#
#	DETAILS for radon
#
#	There are three pairs of data fields for radon. The first two data
#       fields provide the measured concentration value. The second two data
#       fields provide a measure of the uncertainly around the measured value.
#       The third two fields provide the minimum detectable concentration
#       associated with the measured value. For each pair of data fields,
#       the field with a label that begins with "R_", provides
#	a remark code for each data record, and the data field with a label 
#       that begins with "V_", provides the value for each data record. The 
#       prefixes "R_" and "V_" in the data field labels are 
#	followed by a 5-digit number that gives the USGS parameter code 
#	(pcode) for the water-quality parameter. The pcodes in the data field 
#	labels are followed by the name of the water-quality parameter. 
#
#	Remark Field Subdescription:
#
#	Code	Description
#	----	------------------------------------------------------------
#	  <	Not detected. Value reported as less than the Maximum Long-
#		Term Method Detection Level
#
#	  E	Detected. Concentration is estimated. Reasons for estimated 
#		concentration include (Childress and others, 1999):
#		1. Result was below the laboratory reporting level but
#		   above the long-term method detection level, or, for
#		   information-rich methods, result was below the 
#		   long-term method detection level.
#		2. Result was extrapolated above the calibration curve.
#		3. Data quantification was not performed according to 
#		   method-specific criteria.
#		4. Performance of the analyte does not meet acceptable 
#		   method-specific criteria.
#	 	5. Deviation from the standard operating procedure was 
#		   required.
#		6. Some moderate losses occurred in sample preparation 
#	 	   but were not quantifiable.
#		7. Moderate matrix interference conditions occurred.
#
#	  -	No remark (detected)
#
#	  > 	Greater than
#
#	Parameter codes (pcodes) and names with units
#
#	pcode	Parameter names with units
#	----	------------------------------------------------------------
#	82303	Radon-222, water, unfiltered, picocuries per liter
#       76002   Radon-222 2-sigma combined uncertainty, water, unfiltered, 
#               picocuries per liter
#	99327	Radon-222 sample-specific minimum detectable concentration, 
#		water, unfiltered, picocuries per liter
#
#-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#  End METADATA documentation
#
#  BEGIN DATA: pasum_pub.radon.txt
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
